📄 client/src/components/dashboard/TriumphsPanel.tsx (156 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { Search, Trophy, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import type { RecordItem } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';

const appleEase = [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] as const;

function TriumphRecord({ record, index }: { record: RecordItem; index: number }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay: Math.min(index * 0.025, 0.4), duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
      className={`border transition-all duration-300 p-3 will-change-transform ${
        record.completed
          ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E5C76B]/[0.06] to-transparent border-[#E5C76B]/20 hover:border-[#E5C76B]/40'
          : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-white/[0.02] to-transparent border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/15'
      } hover-lift`}
      data-testid={`record-${record.recordHash}`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className={`w-10 h-10 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden ring-1 ${
          record.completed ? 'ring-[#E5C76B]/30' : 'ring-white/10'
        }`}>
          {record.icon ? (
            <img src={record.icon} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" loading="lazy" />
          ) : (
            <Trophy className={`w-5 h-5 ${record.completed ? 'text-[#E5C76B]' : 'text-gray-600'}`} />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h4 className={`text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase truncate ${
              record.completed ? 'text-[#E5C76B]' : 'text-white'
            }`}>{record.name}</h4>
            {record.completed && (
              <div className="shrink-0 w-4 h-4 bg-green-500/80 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-white" />
              </div>
            )}
            {record.scoreValue > 0 && (
              <span className="text-[8px] text-[#E5C76B]/60 shrink-0">{record.scoreValue} pts</span>
            )}
          </div>
          {record.description && (
            <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5 line-clamp-2">{record.description}</p>
          )}
          {record.objectives.length > 0 && !record.completed && (
            <div className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
              {record.objectives.map((obj, i) => {
                const pct = obj.completionValue > 0 ? Math.min(100, (obj.progress / obj.completionValue) * 100) : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={i}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
                      <span className="text-[8px] text-gray-500 truncate flex-1">{obj.description || t('triumphs.progress')}</span>
                      <span className="text-[8px] text-gray-400 shrink-0 ml-2">
                        {obj.progress.toLocaleString()}/{obj.completionValue.toLocaleString()}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-1 bg-white/[0.06] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className={`h-full rounded-full progress-animated ${
                          obj.complete ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'
                        }`}
                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

interface TriumphsPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  triumphsSearch: string;
  setTriumphsSearch: (s: string) => void;
  triumphsFilter: 'all' | 'completed' | 'incomplete';
  setTriumphsFilter: (f: 'all' | 'completed' | 'incomplete') => void;
  filteredRecords: RecordItem[];
}

export function TriumphsPanel({
  tabDirection, triumphsSearch, setTriumphsSearch,
  triumphsFilter, setTriumphsFilter, filteredRecords,
}: TriumphsPanelProps) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <motion.div key="triumphs" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 40 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -40 }} transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }} className="h-full flex flex-col">
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ delay: 0.1, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase }}
        className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-2 shrink-0 space-y-3"
      >
        <div className="relative group">
          <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-500 group-focus-within:text-indigo-400 transition-colors" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder={t('triumphs.search')}
            value={triumphsSearch}
            onChange={(e) => setTriumphsSearch(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-[#0a0e18]/80 border border-white/[0.08] text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500/40 transition-all font-light tracking-wider destiny-clip-sm"
            data-testid="input-search-triumphs"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {(['all', 'completed', 'incomplete'] as const).map(f => (
            <motion.button
              key={f}
              onClick={() => setTriumphsFilter(f)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest border transition-all press-scale ${
                triumphsFilter === f
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600/30 border-indigo-500/50 text-white'
                  : 'bg-white/5 border-white/5 text-gray-500 hover:text-white'
              }`}
              whileTap={{ scale: 0.94 }}
              data-testid={`filter-triumphs-${f}`}
            >
              {f === 'all' ? t('triumphs.all') : f === 'completed' ? t('triumphs.completed') : t('triumphs.incomplete')}
            </motion.button>
          ))}
          <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 ml-2">
            {t('triumphs.score')}: {filteredRecords.filter(r => r.completed).reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.scoreValue, 0).toLocaleString()}
          </span>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 pt-2 scroll-container" data-scroll-container>
        {filteredRecords.length === 0 ? (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-64 space-y-4"
          >
            <Trophy className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-700" />
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{t('item.noneAvailable')}</p>
          </motion.div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {filteredRecords.slice(0, 100).map((record, i) => (
              <TriumphRecord key={record.recordHash} record={record} index={i} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/dashboard/VaultItemCard.tsx (156 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Sparkles, ArrowRightLeft, Shield, Package } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import type { InventoryItem, CharacterData } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { rateWeaponRoll } from '@/lib/roll-ratings';
import { RollRatingBadge } from './EquippedSlotRow';
import { TierStars } from './TierStars';

function getRarityBg(rarity: string): string {
  switch (rarity) {
    case 'exotic': return 'bg-gradient-to-br from-[#ceae33]/30 via-[#ceae33]/10 to-[#ceae33]/5';
    case 'legendary': return 'bg-gradient-to-br from-[#522f65]/40 via-[#522f65]/15 to-[#522f65]/5';
    case 'rare': return 'bg-gradient-to-br from-[#5076a3]/30 via-[#5076a3]/10 to-transparent';
    default: return 'bg-gradient-to-br from-white/[0.06] via-white/[0.02] to-transparent';
  }
}

function getRarityBorder(rarity: string, isInLoadout: boolean): string {
  if (isInLoadout) return 'border-white/5';
  switch (rarity) {
    case 'exotic': return 'border-[#ceae33]/30 hover:border-[#ceae33]/60';
    case 'legendary': return 'border-[#522f65]/40 hover:border-[#522f65]/70';
    case 'rare': return 'border-[#5076a3]/30 hover:border-[#5076a3]/60';
    default: return 'border-white/[0.08] hover:border-white/20';
  }
}

const spring = { type: 'spring' as const, damping: 25, stiffness: 300 };

export function VaultItemCard({ item, characters, onTransferToChar, onEquipOnChar, onAddToLoadout, isInLoadout }: {
  item: InventoryItem;
  characters: CharacterData[];
  onTransferToChar: (charId: string) => void;
  onEquipOnChar: (charId: string) => void;
  onAddToLoadout: () => void;
  isInLoadout: boolean;
}) {
  const [showDropdown, setShowDropdown] = useState(false);
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const isExotic = item.rarity === 'exotic';
  const rarityColor = isExotic ? '#ceae33' : item.rarity === 'legendary' ? '#522f65' : item.rarity === 'rare' ? '#5076a3' : '#333';
  const isWeapon = ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'].includes(item.slot);
  const cardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const imgSrc = item.icon || item.screenshot || '';

  const rollResult = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isWeapon) return null;
    const perkNames = [
      ...(item.perks || []).filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name),
      ...(item.mods || []).map(m => m.name),
    ];
    if (perkNames.length === 0) return null;
    return rateWeaponRoll(item.type, perkNames);
  }, [item, isWeapon]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!showDropdown) return;
    const close = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (!target.closest(`[data-dropdown-id="${item.id}"]`)) setShowDropdown(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('click', close);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('click', close);
  }, [showDropdown, item.id]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={cardRef}
      className={`group relative border transition-all duration-300 rounded-lg ${
        isInLoadout ? 'opacity-30 grayscale-[70%]' : ''
      } ${getRarityBorder(item.rarity, isInLoadout)}`}
      style={{ overflow: showDropdown ? 'visible' : 'hidden' }}
      data-dropdown-id={item.id}
    >
      <div className={`aspect-square relative overflow-hidden rounded-t-lg cursor-pointer ${getRarityBg(item.rarity)}`} onClick={() => setShowDropdown(!showDropdown)}>
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-[2px]" style={{ backgroundColor: rarityColor }} />
        {imgSrc ? (
          <img
            src={imgSrc}
            alt={item.name}
            className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
            loading="lazy"
            onError={(e) => { (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'; (e.target as HTMLImageElement).parentElement?.querySelector('.img-fallback')?.classList.remove('hidden'); }}
          />
        ) : null}
        <div className={`img-fallback w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center absolute inset-0 ${imgSrc ? 'hidden' : ''}`}>
          <Package className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600" />
        </div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-1 left-1 z-10">
          <TierStars rarity={item.rarity} />
        </div>
        {showDropdown && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-indigo-500/[0.08] border-2 border-indigo-400/40 rounded-t-lg" />}
      </div>
      <div className="px-1.5 py-1.5 bg-[#0a0e18]/90 rounded-b-lg">
        <h4 className="text-[8px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase" style={{ color: isExotic ? '#E5C76B' : 'white' }}>{item.name}</h4>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-0.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0">
            <p className="text-[7px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-widest truncate">{item.type}</p>
            {rollResult && <RollRatingBadge result={rollResult} />}
          </div>
          {item.power > 0 && (
            <p className="text-[8px] text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-0.5 shrink-0">
              <Sparkles className="w-2 h-2 text-[#E5C76B]" /> {item.power}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {showDropdown && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8, scale: 0.95 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
            className="absolute left-0 right-0 bottom-0 z-30 bg-[#0c1020]/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-indigo-500/20 shadow-xl shadow-black/60 rounded-b-lg"
          >
            <div className="py-1">
              <motion.button
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.05, duration: 0.2 }}
                onClick={() => { onAddToLoadout(); setShowDropdown(false); }}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-[#E5C76B] hover:bg-yellow-500/10 transition-all press-scale"
                data-testid={`dropdown-loadout-${item.id}`}>
                <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" /> {t('action.toLoadout')}
              </motion.button>
              <div className="h-px bg-white/[0.04] mx-2" />
              {characters.map((c, i) => (
                <motion.div
                  key={c.characterId}
                  className="flex items-center"
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -8 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: 0.08 + i * 0.04, duration: 0.2 }}
                >
                  <button onClick={() => { onTransferToChar(c.characterId); setShowDropdown(false); }}
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/[0.06] transition-all press-scale"
                    data-testid={`dropdown-transfer-${item.id}-${c.characterId}`}>
                    <ArrowRightLeft className="w-3 h-3" /> {c.className}
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { onEquipOnChar(c.characterId); setShowDropdown(false); }}
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-indigo-500/15 transition-all press-scale" title={t('action.equip')}
                    data-testid={`dropdown-equip-${item.id}-${c.characterId}`}>
                    <Shield className="w-3 h-3" />
                  </button>
                </motion.div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/dashboard/VaultPanel.tsx (137 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { Search, Package, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import type { InventoryItem, CharacterData } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import type { Item, SlotInfo } from '@/components/Inventory';
import { EquippedSlot } from '@/components/Inventory';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { FilterState } from './shared';
import { FilterBar } from './FilterBar';
import { VaultItemCard } from './VaultItemCard';
import { toItem } from './shared';

const appleEase = [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] as const;

interface VaultPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  vaultFilters: FilterState;
  setVaultFilters: (fn: (f: FilterState) => FilterState) => void;
  filteredVaultItems: InventoryItem[];
  characters: CharacterData[];
  loadout: Record<string, Item>;
  addToLoadout: (item: Item) => void;
  unequipItem: (slotId: string) => void;
  handleTransfer: (item: InventoryItem, toVault: boolean, characterId: string) => void;
  handleEquip: (item: InventoryItem, characterId: string) => void;
  setIsSaveModalOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  ITEM_SLOTS: SlotInfo[];
  SLOT_FILTERS: { id: string; label: string }[];
  RARITY_FILTERS: { id: string; label: string }[];
}

export function VaultPanel({
  tabDirection, vaultFilters, setVaultFilters, filteredVaultItems, characters,
  loadout, addToLoadout, unequipItem, handleTransfer, handleEquip,
  setIsSaveModalOpen, ITEM_SLOTS, SLOT_FILTERS, RARITY_FILTERS,
}: VaultPanelProps) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <motion.div key="vault" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 40 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -40 }} transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }} className="h-full flex flex-col lg:flex-row">
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 border-r border-white/5 min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.1, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase }}
          className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-2 shrink-0 space-y-3"
        >
          <div className="relative group focus-glow rounded-sm">
            <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-500 group-focus-within:text-indigo-400 transition-colors" />
            <input 
              type="text"
              placeholder={t('vault.search')}
              value={vaultFilters.search}
              onChange={(e) => setVaultFilters(f => ({ ...f, search: e.target.value }))}
              className="w-full bg-[#0a0e18]/80 border border-white/[0.08] text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500/40 transition-all font-light tracking-wider destiny-clip-sm"
              data-testid="input-search-vault"
            />
          </div>
          <FilterBar filters={vaultFilters} onChange={(f) => setVaultFilters(() => f)} slotFilters={SLOT_FILTERS} rarityFilters={RARITY_FILTERS} />
          <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 tracking-widest uppercase">{filteredVaultItems.length} Gegenstande</p>
        </motion.div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 pt-2 scroll-container" data-scroll-container>
          {filteredVaultItems.length === 0 ? (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase }}
              className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-64 space-y-4"
            >
              <Package className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-700" />
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                {vaultFilters.search || vaultFilters.slot !== 'all' || vaultFilters.rarity !== 'all'
                  ? t('item.noneAvailable')
                  : t('item.noneAvailable')}
              </p>
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-7 gap-2 md:gap-3">
              {filteredVaultItems.map((item, i) => (
                <motion.div
                  key={item.id}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 16, scale: 0.95 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
                  transition={{ delay: Math.min(i * 0.02, 0.4), duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
                >
                  <VaultItemCard
                    item={item}
                    characters={characters}
                    onTransferToChar={(charId) => handleTransfer(item, false, charId)}
                    onEquipOnChar={(charId) => handleEquip(item, charId)}
                    onAddToLoadout={() => addToLoadout(toItem(item))}
                    isInLoadout={loadout[item.slot]?.id === item.id}
                  />
                </motion.div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
        transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase }}
        className="hidden lg:flex w-[380px] bg-[#0a0e18]/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex-col shrink-0 relative overflow-hidden"
      >
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-indigo-500/5 blur-[60px] pointer-events-none animate-breathe" />
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-white font-display font-light tracking-[0.2em] flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#E5C76B]" /> AKTIVES LOADOUT
          </h3>
          <motion.button
            onClick={() => setIsSaveModalOpen(true)}
            disabled={Object.keys(loadout).length === 0}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white text-white hover:text-black transition-all duration-500 disabled:opacity-20 border border-white/10 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] destiny-clip-sm"
            whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
            data-testid="button-save-loadout"
          >
            SPEICHERN
          </motion.button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-3 scroll-container">
          {ITEM_SLOTS.map((slot, i) => (
            <motion.div
              key={slot.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 15 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.4 + i * 0.05, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
            >
              <EquippedSlot slotInfo={slot} item={loadout[slot.id]} onUnequip={() => unequipItem(slot.id)} />
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/dashboard/WeaponRollPanel.tsx (536 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Crosshair, Search, Loader2, AlertTriangle, ChevronRight, Star, ChevronDown, RefreshCw, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { InventoryItem } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { apiRequest } from '@/lib/queryClient';

function bungieIcon(url: string | undefined): string {
  if (!url) return '';
  if (url.startsWith('http')) return url;
  return `https://www.bungie.net${url}`;
}

interface PerkBreakdown {
  name: string;
  rating: 's' | 'a' | 'b' | 'c' | 'f';
  explanation: string;
}

interface WeaponRollEvaluation {
  verdict: 'god_roll' | 'great' | 'good' | 'average' | 'shard';
  score: number;
  perkBreakdown: PerkBreakdown[];
  idealPerks: string[];
  summary: string;
}

interface WeaponRollPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  vaultItems: InventoryItem[];
  characterItems: InventoryItem[];
}

const appleEase = [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] as const;

const tabVariants = {
  enter: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? 60 : -60, opacity: 0 }),
  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

const WEAPON_SLOTS = ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'];

const verdictConfig: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; bgColor: string; borderColor: string }> = {
  god_roll: { label: 'GOD ROLL', color: '#E5C76B', bgColor: 'rgba(229, 199, 107, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(229, 199, 107, 0.3)' },
  great: { label: 'GREAT', color: '#818cf8', bgColor: 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.3)' },
  good: { label: 'GOOD', color: '#34d399', bgColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.3)' },
  average: { label: 'AVERAGE', color: '#f59e0b', bgColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3)' },
  shard: { label: 'SHARD', color: '#ef4444', bgColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)' },
};

const ratingColors: Record<string, string> = {
  s: '#E5C76B',
  a: '#818cf8',
  b: '#34d399',
  c: '#f59e0b',
  f: '#ef4444',
};

function RatingRing({ rating, size = 96 }: { rating: number; size?: number }) {
  const strokeWidth = 6;
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const progress = (rating / 10) * circumference;
  const color = rating >= 8 ? '#E5C76B' : rating >= 6 ? '#818cf8' : rating >= 4 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';

  return (
    <div className="relative" style={{ width: size, height: size }} data-testid="rating-ring-weapon">
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="white" strokeOpacity={0.06} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
        <motion.circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round"
          initial={{ strokeDasharray: circumference, strokeDashoffset: circumference }}
          animate={{ strokeDashoffset: circumference - progress }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <motion.span
          className="text-2xl font-bold text-white"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.5, type: 'spring', damping: 20, stiffness: 200 }}
        >
          {rating}
        </motion.span>
        <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">/10</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function WeaponRollPanel({ tabDirection, vaultItems, characterItems }: WeaponRollPanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [selectedWeapon, setSelectedWeapon] = useState<InventoryItem | null>(null);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<WeaponRollEvaluation | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<string, WeaponRollEvaluation>>({});

  const allWeapons = useMemo(() => {
    const weapons = [...characterItems, ...vaultItems].filter(
      item => WEAPON_SLOTS.includes(item.slot) && (item.rarity === 'legendary' || item.rarity === 'exotic')
    );
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    return weapons.filter(w => {
      if (seen.has(w.id)) return false;
      seen.add(w.id);
      return true;
    });
  }, [vaultItems, characterItems]);

  const filteredWeapons = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery) return allWeapons;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return allWeapons.filter(w =>
      w.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || w.type.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [allWeapons, searchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const evaluateRoll = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedWeapon) return;

    if (cache[selectedWeapon.id]) {
      setEvaluation(cache[selectedWeapon.id]);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const weaponData = {
      name: selectedWeapon.name,
      type: selectedWeapon.type,
      slot: selectedWeapon.slot,
      rarity: selectedWeapon.rarity,
      power: selectedWeapon.power,
      perks: (selectedWeapon.perks || []).map((p, idx) => ({
        name: p.name,
        isActive: p.isActive,
        column: idx,
      })),
      mods: (selectedWeapon.mods || []).map(m => ({ name: m.name })),
      masterwork: '',
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiRequest('POST', '/api/ai/evaluate-roll', { weaponData, language });
      const data = await res.json();
      setEvaluation(data);
      setCache(prev => ({ ...prev, [selectedWeapon.id]: data }));
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message?.includes('429') || err.message?.includes('rate limit')) {
        setError(t('analysis.rateLimit'));
      } else {
        setError(t('analysis.error'));
      }
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedWeapon, language, t, cache]);

  const selectWeapon = useCallback((weapon: InventoryItem) => {
    setSelectedWeapon(weapon);
    setIsDropdownOpen(false);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setEvaluation(null);
    setError(null);

    if (cache[weapon.id]) {
      setEvaluation(cache[weapon.id]);
    }
  }, [cache]);

  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedWeapon(null);
    setEvaluation(null);
    setError(null);
  }, []);

  return (
    <motion.div
      key="weapon-roll"
      custom={tabDirection}
      variants={tabVariants}
      initial="enter"
      animate="center"
      exit="exit"
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="h-full overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden scroll-container px-4 md:px-8 py-6"
      data-scroll-container
      data-testid="panel-weapon-roll"
    >
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"
        >
          <Crosshair className="w-5 h-5 text-orange-400" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-[0.2em] text-white uppercase" data-testid="text-weapon-roll-title">{t('roll.title')}</h2>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
          className="space-y-4"
        >
          <div ref={dropdownRef} className="relative">
            <div
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 bg-white/[0.03] border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? 'border-orange-500/40' : 'border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/[0.12]'}`}
              onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(!isDropdownOpen)}
              data-testid="dropdown-weapon-selector"
            >
              {selectedWeapon ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
                  {selectedWeapon.icon && (
                    <img
                      src={bungieIcon(selectedWeapon.icon)}
                      alt=""
                      className="w-8 h-8 rounded"
                    />
                  )}
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm text-white truncate" data-testid="text-selected-weapon">{selectedWeapon.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{selectedWeapon.type} · {selectedWeapon.power}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); clearSelection(); }}
                    className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                    data-testid="button-clear-weapon"
                  >
                    <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1">
                  <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">{t('roll.selectWeapon')}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-gray-500 transition-transform duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
            </div>

            <AnimatePresence>
              {isDropdownOpen && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                  className="absolute z-50 w-full mt-2 bg-[#0d1117] border border-white/[0.08] rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-h-72 flex flex-col"
                >
                  <div className="p-2 border-b border-white/[0.06]">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/[0.04] rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                      <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={searchQuery}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder={t('roll.searchWeapons')}
                        className="bg-transparent text-sm text-white placeholder-gray-500 outline-none flex-1"
                        autoFocus
                        data-testid="input-weapon-search"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="overflow-y-auto scroll-container flex-1">
                    {filteredWeapons.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-gray-500">
                        {t('item.noneAvailable')}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      filteredWeapons.map((weapon) => (
                        <button
                          key={weapon.id}
                          onClick={() => selectWeapon(weapon)}
                          className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-white/[0.04] ${selectedWeapon?.id === weapon.id ? 'bg-white/[0.06]' : ''}`}
                          data-testid={`option-weapon-${weapon.id}`}
                        >
                          {weapon.icon && (
                            <img
                              src={bungieIcon(weapon.icon)}
                              alt=""
                              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded ${weapon.rarity === 'exotic' ? 'ring-1 ring-[#E5C76B]/30' : ''}`}
                            />
                          )}
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-200 truncate">{weapon.name}</p>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{weapon.type} · {weapon.power}</p>
                          </div>
                          <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider ${weapon.rarity === 'exotic' ? 'text-[#E5C76B]/60' : 'text-purple-400/60'}`}>
                            {weapon.rarity}
                          </span>
                          {cache[weapon.id] && (
                            <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400/60" />
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ))
                    )}
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          {selectedWeapon && !evaluation && !isLoading && (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
              className="flex flex-col items-center py-10 space-y-4"
            >
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Crosshair className="w-8 h-8 text-orange-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm text-center max-w-sm">{t('roll.description')}</p>
              <button
                onClick={evaluateRoll}
                className="press-scale px-8 py-3 bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-display tracking-[0.2em] uppercase transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm border border-orange-500/30"
                data-testid="button-evaluate-roll"
              >
                {t('roll.evaluate')}
              </button>
              {error && (
                <motion.p
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  className="text-red-400 text-xs flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {error}
                </motion.p>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {!selectedWeapon && !isLoading && (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
            >
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center animate-breathe">
                <Crosshair className="w-10 h-10 text-orange-400" />
              </div>
              <div className="text-center space-y-2">
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm max-w-md">{t('roll.description')}</p>
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs">{allWeapons.length} {t('roll.weaponsAvailable')}</p>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </motion.div>

        {isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-orange-400 animate-spin" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full border border-orange-500/20 animate-ping" style={{ animationDuration: '2s' }} />
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-display tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">{t('roll.loading')}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {evaluation && !isLoading && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className={`bg-white/[0.03] border rounded-xl p-6 hover-lift ${evaluation.verdict === 'god_roll' ? 'godroll-glow' : 'border-white/[0.06]'}`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                <RatingRing rating={evaluation.score} />
                <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap"
                  >
                    <span
                      className="inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-display tracking-[0.15em] uppercase"
                      style={{
                        color: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.color,
                        backgroundColor: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.bgColor,
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        borderColor: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.borderColor,
                      }}
                      data-testid="badge-verdict"
                    >
                      {verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.label || evaluation.verdict}
                    </span>
                    {selectedWeapon && (
                      <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs">{selectedWeapon.name}</span>
                    )}
                  </motion.div>
                  <motion.p
                    initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }}
                    className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed"
                    data-testid="text-roll-summary"
                  >
                    {evaluation.summary}
                  </motion.p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.2 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <Crosshair className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-[0.15em] text-orange-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-perk-breakdown-title">{t('roll.perkBreakdown')}</h3>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-3" data-testid="list-perk-breakdown">
                {evaluation.perkBreakdown.map((perk, i) => (
                  <motion.div
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-white/[0.02] rounded-lg border border-white/[0.04]"
                    data-testid={`perk-breakdown-${i}`}
                  >
                    <span
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-md text-xs font-bold uppercase shrink-0"
                      style={{
                        color: ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888',
                        backgroundColor: `${ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888'}15`,
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        borderColor: `${ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888'}30`,
                      }}
                      data-testid={`badge-perk-rating-${i}`}
                    >
                      {perk.rating.toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className="text-sm text-white font-medium" data-testid={`text-perk-name-${i}`}>{perk.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">{perk.explanation}</p>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#E5C76B]" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-[0.15em] text-[#E5C76B] uppercase" data-testid="text-ideal-perks-title">{t('roll.idealPerks')}</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-ideal-perks">
                {evaluation.idealPerks.map((perk, i) => (
                  <motion.li
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.4 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                  >
                    <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E5C76B]/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    {perk}
                  </motion.li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2 pb-8 flex-wrap"
            >
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  if (selectedWeapon) {
                    setCache(prev => {
                      const next = { ...prev };
                      delete next[selectedWeapon.id];
                      return next;
                    });
                    setEvaluation(null);
                  }
                }}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-[0.15em] uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-reevaluate"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('roll.reevaluate')}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={clearSelection}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-[0.15em] uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-select-another"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('roll.selectAnother')}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/dashboard/shared.ts (87 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { InventoryItem } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import type { Item } from '@/components/Inventory';

export type TabType = 'characters' | 'vault' | 'loadouts' | 'postmaster' | 'collections' | 'triumphs' | 'season' | 'ai' | 'news';

export type AiSubTab = 'playstyle' | 'build' | 'roll' | 'triumph' | 'synergy';

export interface FilterState {
  search: string;
  slot: string;
  rarity: string;
  minPower: string;
  maxPower: string;
}

export function toItem(inv: InventoryItem): Item {
  const item: Item = {
    id: inv.id,
    itemHash: inv.itemHash,
    name: inv.name,
    type: inv.type,
    slot: inv.slot,
    screenshot: inv.screenshot,
    rarity: (inv.rarity === 'exotic' || inv.rarity === 'legendary') ? inv.rarity : 'legendary',
    power: inv.power,
    icon: inv.icon,
  };

  if (inv.perks && inv.perks.length > 0) {
    item.perks = inv.perks.filter(p => p.name).map(p => ({
      perkHash: p.perkHash,
      name: p.name,
      icon: p.icon,
      isActive: p.isActive,
    }));
  }

  if (inv.mods && inv.mods.length > 0) {
    item.mods = inv.mods.filter(m => m.name).map(m => ({
      plugHash: m.plugHash,
      name: m.name,
      icon: m.icon,
    }));
  }

  if (inv.stats && inv.stats.length > 0) {
    item.stats = inv.stats.map(s => ({
      statHash: s.statHash,
      name: s.name,
      value: s.value,
    }));
  }

  if (inv.sockets && inv.sockets.length > 0 && inv.slot === 'subclass') {
    const SOCKET_CATS: Record<number, string> = {
      0: 'super', 1: 'classAbility', 2: 'movement', 3: 'melee', 4: 'grenade',
      5: 'aspect', 6: 'aspect', 7: 'fragment', 8: 'fragment', 9: 'fragment', 10: 'fragment', 11: 'fragment',
    };
    item.sockets = inv.sockets.filter(s => s.name).map(s => ({
      socketIndex: s.socketIndex,
      plugHash: s.plugHash,
      name: s.name,
      icon: s.icon,
      description: s.description,
      category: SOCKET_CATS[s.socketIndex] || 'other',
    }));
  }

  return item;
}

export function useItemFilter(items: InventoryItem[], filters: FilterState): InventoryItem[] {
  return useMemo(() => {
    return items.filter(item => {
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        if (!item.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !item.type.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
      }
      if (filters.slot !== 'all' && item.slot !== filters.slot) return false;
      if (filters.rarity !== 'all' && item.rarity !== filters.rarity) return false;
      if (filters.minPower && item.power < parseInt(filters.minPower)) return false;
      if (filters.maxPower && item.power > parseInt(filters.maxPower)) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [items, filters]);
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0" +
  " hover-elevate active-elevate-2",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border border-primary-border",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground border border-destructive-border",
        outline:
          // Shows the background color of whatever card / sidebar / accent background it is inside of.
          // Inherits the current text color.
          " border [border-color:var(--button-outline)]  shadow-xs active:shadow-none ",
        secondary: "border bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground border border-secondary-border ",
        // Add a transparent border so that when someone toggles a border on later, it doesn't shift layout/size.
        ghost: "border border-transparent",
      },
      // Heights are set as "min" heights, because sometimes Ai will place large amount of content
      // inside buttons. With a min-height they will look appropriate with small amounts of content,
      // but will expand to fit large amounts of content.
      size: {
        default: "min-h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "min-h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "min-h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  },
)

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
    return (
      <Comp
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
        ref={ref}
        {...props}
      />
    )
  },
)
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { Button, buttonVariants }

📄 client/src/components/ui/carousel.tsx (260 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import * as React from "react"
import useEmblaCarousel, {
  type UseEmblaCarouselType,
} from "embla-carousel-react"
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

type CarouselApi = UseEmblaCarouselType[1]
type UseCarouselParameters = Parameters<typeof useEmblaCarousel>
type CarouselOptions = UseCarouselParameters[0]
type CarouselPlugin = UseCarouselParameters[1]

type CarouselProps = {
  opts?: CarouselOptions
  plugins?: CarouselPlugin
  orientation?: "horizontal" | "vertical"
  setApi?: (api: CarouselApi) => void
}

type CarouselContextProps = {
  carouselRef: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[0]
  api: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[1]
  scrollPrev: () => void
  scrollNext: () => void
  canScrollPrev: boolean
  canScrollNext: boolean
} & CarouselProps

const CarouselContext = React.createContext<CarouselContextProps | null>(null)

function useCarousel() {
  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext)

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />")
  }

  return context
}

const Carousel = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & CarouselProps
>(
  (
    {
      orientation = "horizontal",
      opts,
      setApi,
      plugins,
      className,
      children,
      ...props
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel(
      {
        ...opts,
        axis: orientation === "horizontal" ? "x" : "y",
      },
      plugins
    )
    const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false)
    const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false)

    const onSelect = React.useCallback((api: CarouselApi) => {
      if (!api) {
        return
      }

      setCanScrollPrev(api.canScrollPrev())
      setCanScrollNext(api.canScrollNext())
    }, [])

    const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => {
      api?.scrollPrev()
    }, [api])

    const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => {
      api?.scrollNext()
    }, [api])

    const handleKeyDown = React.useCallback(
      (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
        if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
          event.preventDefault()
          scrollPrev()
        } else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
          event.preventDefault()
          scrollNext()
        }
      },
      [scrollPrev, scrollNext]
    )

    React.useEffect(() => {
      if (!api || !setApi) {
        return
      }

      setApi(api)
    }, [api, setApi])

    React.useEffect(() => {
      if (!api) {
        return
      }

      onSelect(api)
      api.on("reInit", onSelect)
      api.on("select", onSelect)

      return () => {
        api?.off("select", onSelect)
      }
    }, [api, onSelect])

    return (
      <CarouselContext.Provider
        value={{
          carouselRef,
          api: api,
          opts,
          orientation:
            orientation || (opts?.axis === "y" ? "vertical" : "horizontal"),
          scrollPrev,
          scrollNext,
          canScrollPrev,
          canScrollNext,
        }}
      >
        <div
          ref={ref}
          onKeyDownCapture={handleKeyDown}
          className={cn("relative", className)}
          role="region"
          aria-roledescription="carousel"
          {...props}
        >
          {children}
        </div>
      </CarouselContext.Provider>
    )
  }
)
Carousel.displayName = "Carousel"

const CarouselContent = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { carouselRef, orientation } = useCarousel()

  return (
    <div ref={carouselRef} className="overflow-hidden">
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "flex",
          orientation === "horizontal" ? "-ml-4" : "-mt-4 flex-col",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    </div>
  )
})
CarouselContent.displayName = "CarouselContent"

const CarouselItem = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation } = useCarousel()

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      role="group"
      aria-roledescription="slide"
      className={cn(
        "min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full",
        orientation === "horizontal" ? "pl-4" : "pt-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
CarouselItem.displayName = "CarouselItem"

const CarouselPrevious = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLButtonElement,
  React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>
>(({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollPrev, canScrollPrev } = useCarousel()

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn(
        "absolute  h-8 w-8 rounded-full",
        orientation === "horizontal"
          ? "-left-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
          : "-top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90",
        className
      )}
      disabled={!canScrollPrev}
      onClick={scrollPrev}
      {...props}
    >
      <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Previous slide</span>
    </Button>
  )
})
CarouselPrevious.displayName = "CarouselPrevious"

const CarouselNext = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLButtonElement,
  React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>
>(({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollNext, canScrollNext } = useCarousel()

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn(
        "absolute h-8 w-8 rounded-full",
        orientation === "horizontal"
          ? "-right-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
          : "-bottom-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90",
        className
      )}
      disabled={!canScrollNext}
      onClick={scrollNext}
      {...props}
    >
      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Next slide</span>
    </Button>
  )
})
CarouselNext.displayName = "CarouselNext"

export {
  type CarouselApi,
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselItem,
  CarouselPrevious,
  CarouselNext,
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/ui/chart.tsx (365 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as RechartsPrimitive from "recharts"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Format: { THEME_NAME: CSS_SELECTOR }
const THEMES = { light: "", dark: ".dark" } as const

export type ChartConfig = {
  [k in string]: {
    label?: React.ReactNode
    icon?: React.ComponentType
  } & (
    | { color?: string; theme?: never }
    | { color?: never; theme: Record<keyof typeof THEMES, string> }
  )
}

type ChartContextProps = {
  config: ChartConfig
}

const ChartContext = React.createContext<ChartContextProps | null>(null)

function useChart() {
  const context = React.useContext(ChartContext)

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useChart must be used within a <ChartContainer />")
  }

  return context
}

const ChartContainer = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
    config: ChartConfig
    children: React.ComponentProps<
      typeof RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer
    >["children"]
  }
>(({ id, className, children, config, ...props }, ref) => {
  const uniqueId = React.useId()
  const chartId = `chart-${id || uniqueId.replace(/:/g, "")}`

  return (
    <ChartContext.Provider value={{ config }}>
      <div
        data-chart={chartId}
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "flex aspect-video justify-center text-xs [&_.recharts-cartesian-axis-tick_text]:fill-muted-foreground [&_.recharts-cartesian-grid_line[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border/50 [&_.recharts-curve.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-dot[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-layer]:outline-none [&_.recharts-polar-grid_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-radial-bar-background-sector]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-rectangle.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-reference-line_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-sector[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-sector]:outline-none [&_.recharts-surface]:outline-none",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        <ChartStyle id={chartId} config={config} />
        <RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
          {children}
        </RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartContext.Provider>
  )
})
ChartContainer.displayName = "Chart"

const ChartStyle = ({ id, config }: { id: string; config: ChartConfig }) => {
  const colorConfig = Object.entries(config).filter(
    ([, config]) => config.theme || config.color
  )

  if (!colorConfig.length) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <style
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
        __html: Object.entries(THEMES)
          .map(
            ([theme, prefix]) => `
${prefix} [data-chart=${id}] {
${colorConfig
  .map(([key, itemConfig]) => {
    const color =
      itemConfig.theme?.[theme as keyof typeof itemConfig.theme] ||
      itemConfig.color
    return color ? `  --color-${key}: ${color};` : null
  })
  .join("\n")}
}
`
          )
          .join("\n"),
      }}
    />
  )
}

const ChartTooltip = RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip

const ChartTooltipContent = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.ComponentProps<typeof RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip> &
    React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
      hideLabel?: boolean
      hideIndicator?: boolean
      indicator?: "line" | "dot" | "dashed"
      nameKey?: string
      labelKey?: string
    }
>(
  (
    {
      active,
      payload,
      className,
      indicator = "dot",
      hideLabel = false,
      hideIndicator = false,
      label,
      labelFormatter,
      labelClassName,
      formatter,
      color,
      nameKey,
      labelKey,
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const { config } = useChart()

    const tooltipLabel = React.useMemo(() => {
      if (hideLabel || !payload?.length) {
        return null
      }

      const [item] = payload
      const key = `${labelKey || item?.dataKey || item?.name || "value"}`
      const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
      const value =
        !labelKey && typeof label === "string"
          ? config[label as keyof typeof config]?.label || label
          : itemConfig?.label

      if (labelFormatter) {
        return (
          <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>
            {labelFormatter(value, payload)}
          </div>
        )
      }

      if (!value) {
        return null
      }

      return <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>{value}</div>
    }, [
      label,
      labelFormatter,
      payload,
      hideLabel,
      labelClassName,
      config,
      labelKey,
    ])

    if (!active || !payload?.length) {
      return null
    }

    const nestLabel = payload.length === 1 && indicator !== "dot"

    return (
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "grid min-w-[8rem] items-start gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-background px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs shadow-xl",
          className
        )}
      >
        {!nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          {payload.map((item, index) => {
            const key = `${nameKey || item.name || item.dataKey || "value"}`
            const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
            const indicatorColor = color || item.payload.fill || item.color

            return (
              <div
                key={item.dataKey}
                className={cn(
                  "flex w-full flex-wrap items-stretch gap-2 [&>svg]:h-2.5 [&>svg]:w-2.5 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground",
                  indicator === "dot" && "items-center"
                )}
              >
                {formatter && item?.value !== undefined && item.name ? (
                  formatter(item.value, item.name, item, index, item.payload)
                ) : (
                  <>
                    {itemConfig?.icon ? (
                      <itemConfig.icon />
                    ) : (
                      !hideIndicator && (
                        <div
                          className={cn(
                            "shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border-[--color-border] bg-[--color-bg]",
                            {
                              "h-2.5 w-2.5": indicator === "dot",
                              "w-1": indicator === "line",
                              "w-0 border-[1.5px] border-dashed bg-transparent":
                                indicator === "dashed",
                              "my-0.5": nestLabel && indicator === "dashed",
                            }
                          )}
                          style={
                            {
                              "--color-bg": indicatorColor,
                              "--color-border": indicatorColor,
                            } as React.CSSProperties
                          }
                        />
                      )
                    )}
                    <div
                      className={cn(
                        "flex flex-1 justify-between leading-none",
                        nestLabel ? "items-end" : "items-center"
                      )}
                    >
                      <div className="grid gap-1.5">
                        {nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                          {itemConfig?.label || item.name}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {item.value && (
                        <span className="font-mono font-medium tabular-nums text-foreground">
                          {item.value.toLocaleString()}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }
)
ChartTooltipContent.displayName = "ChartTooltip"

const ChartLegend = RechartsPrimitive.Legend

const ChartLegendContent = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.ComponentProps<"div"> &
    Pick<RechartsPrimitive.LegendProps, "payload" | "verticalAlign"> & {
      hideIcon?: boolean
      nameKey?: string
    }
>(
  (
    { className, hideIcon = false, payload, verticalAlign = "bottom", nameKey },
    ref
  ) => {
    const { config } = useChart()

    if (!payload?.length) {
      return null
    }

    return (
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "flex items-center justify-center gap-4",
          verticalAlign === "top" ? "pb-3" : "pt-3",
          className
        )}
      >
        {payload.map((item) => {
          const key = `${nameKey || item.dataKey || "value"}`
          const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)

          return (
            <div
              key={item.value}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center gap-1.5 [&>svg]:h-3 [&>svg]:w-3 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground"
              )}
            >
              {itemConfig?.icon && !hideIcon ? (
                <itemConfig.icon />
              ) : (
                <div
                  className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-[2px]"
                  style={{
                    backgroundColor: item.color,
                  }}
                />
              )}
              {itemConfig?.label}
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    )
  }
)
ChartLegendContent.displayName = "ChartLegend"

// Helper to extract item config from a payload.
function getPayloadConfigFromPayload(
  config: ChartConfig,
  payload: unknown,
  key: string
) {
  if (typeof payload !== "object" || payload === null) {
    return undefined
  }

  const payloadPayload =
    "payload" in payload &&
    typeof payload.payload === "object" &&
    payload.payload !== null
      ? payload.payload
      : undefined

  let configLabelKey: string = key

  if (
    key in payload &&
    typeof payload[key as keyof typeof payload] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payload[key as keyof typeof payload] as string
  } else if (
    payloadPayload &&
    key in payloadPayload &&
    typeof payloadPayload[key as keyof typeof payloadPayload] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payloadPayload[
      key as keyof typeof payloadPayload
    ] as string
  }

  return configLabelKey in config
    ? config[configLabelKey]
    : config[key as keyof typeof config]
}

export {
  ChartContainer,
  ChartTooltip,
  ChartTooltipContent,
  ChartLegend,
  ChartLegendContent,
  ChartStyle,
}

const ContextMenuRadioItem = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof ContextMenuPrimitive.RadioItem>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof ContextMenuPrimitive.RadioItem>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ContextMenuPrimitive.RadioItem
    ref={ref}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
📄 client/src/components/ui/form.tsx (178 lines)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as LabelPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-label"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import {
  Controller,
  FormProvider,
  useFormContext,
  type ControllerProps,
  type FieldPath,
  type FieldValues,
} from "react-hook-form"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"

const Form = FormProvider

type FormFieldContextValue<
  TFieldValues extends FieldValues = FieldValues,
  TName extends FieldPath<TFieldValues> = FieldPath<TFieldValues>
> = {
  name: TName
}

const FormFieldContext = React.createContext<FormFieldContextValue>(
  {} as FormFieldContextValue
)

const FormField = <
  TFieldValues extends FieldValues = FieldValues,
  TName extends FieldPath<TFieldValues> = FieldPath<TFieldValues>
>({
  ...props
}: ControllerProps<TFieldValues, TName>) => {
  return (
    <FormFieldContext.Provider value={{ name: props.name }}>
      <Controller {...props} />
    </FormFieldContext.Provider>
  )
}

const useFormField = () => {
  const fieldContext = React.useContext(FormFieldContext)
  const itemContext = React.useContext(FormItemContext)
  const { getFieldState, formState } = useFormContext()

  const fieldState = getFieldState(fieldContext.name, formState)

  if (!fieldContext) {
    throw new Error("useFormField should be used within <FormField>")
  }

  const { id } = itemContext

  return {
    id,
    name: fieldContext.name,
    formItemId: `${id}-form-item`,
    formDescriptionId: `${id}-form-item-description`,
    formMessageId: `${id}-form-item-message`,
    ...fieldState,
  }
}

type FormItemContextValue = {
  id: string
}

const FormItemContext = React.createContext<FormItemContextValue>(
  {} as FormItemContextValue
)

const FormItem = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const id = React.useId()

  return (
    <FormItemContext.Provider value={{ id }}>
      <div ref={ref} className={cn("space-y-2", className)} {...props} />
    </FormItemContext.Provider>
  )
})
FormItem.displayName = "FormItem"

const FormLabel = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof LabelPrimitive.Root>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof LabelPrimitive.Root>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId } = useFormField()

  return (
    <Label
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(error && "text-destructive", className)}
      htmlFor={formItemId}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
FormLabel.displayName = "FormLabel"

const FormControl = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof Slot>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof Slot>
>(({ ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId, formDescriptionId, formMessageId } = useFormField()

  return (
    <Slot
      ref={ref}
      id={formItemId}
      aria-describedby={
        !error
          ? `${formDescriptionId}`
          : `${formDescriptionId} ${formMessageId}`
      }
      aria-invalid={!!error}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
FormControl.displayName = "FormControl"

const FormDescription = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { formDescriptionId } = useFormField()

  return (
    <p
      ref={ref}
      id={formDescriptionId}
      className={cn("text-sm text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
FormDescription.displayName = "FormDescription"

const FormMessage = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formMessageId } = useFormField()
  const body = error ? String(error?.message ?? "") : children

  if (!body) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <p
      ref={ref}
      id={formMessageId}
      className={cn("text-sm font-medium text-destructive", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {body}
    </p>
  )
})
FormMessage.displayName = "FormMessage"

const InputOTPSlot = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<"div">,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"div"> & { index: number }
>(({ index, className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const inputOTPContext = React.useContext(OTPInputContext)
  const { char, hasFakeCaret, isActive } = inputOTPContext.slots[index]

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "relative flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center border-y border-r border-input text-sm transition-all first:rounded-l-md first:border-l last:rounded-r-md",
        isActive && "z-10 ring-2 ring-ring ring-offset-background",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {char}
      {hasFakeCaret && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="h-4 w-px animate-caret-blink bg-foreground duration-1000" />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
})
InputOTPSlot.displayName = "InputOTPSlot"

const InputOTPSeparator = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<"div">,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"div">
>(({ ...props }, ref) => (
  <div ref={ref} role="separator" {...props}>
    <Dot />
  </div>
))
InputOTPSeparator.displayName = "InputOTPSeparator"

  "fixed z-50 gap-4 bg-background p-6 shadow-lg transition ease-in-out data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:duration-300 data-[state=open]:duration-500",
  {
    variants: {
      side: {
        top: "inset-x-0 top-0 border-b data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-top data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-top",
        bottom:
          "inset-x-0 bottom-0 border-t data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-bottom data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-bottom",
        left: "inset-y-0 left-0 h-full w-3/4 border-r data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-left data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-left sm:max-w-sm",
        right:
          "inset-y-0 right-0 h-full w-3/4  border-l data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-right data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-right sm:max-w-sm",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      side: "right",
    },
  }
)

interface SheetContentProps
  extends React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Content>,
    VariantProps<typeof sheetVariants> {}

const SheetContent = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Content>,
  SheetContentProps
>(({ side = "right", className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <SheetPortal>
    <SheetOverlay />
    <SheetPrimitive.Content
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(sheetVariants({ side }), className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <SheetPrimitive.Close className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-sm opacity-70 ring-offset-background transition-opacity hover:opacity-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none data-[state=open]:bg-secondary">
        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
        <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
      </SheetPrimitive.Close>
    </SheetPrimitive.Content>
  </SheetPortal>
))
SheetContent.displayName = SheetPrimitive.Content.displayName

const SheetHeader = ({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
  <div
    className={cn(
      "flex flex-col space-y-2 text-center sm:text-left",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
)
SheetHeader.displayName = "SheetHeader"

const SheetFooter = ({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
  <div
    className={cn(
      "flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row sm:justify-end sm:space-x-2",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
)
SheetFooter.displayName = "SheetFooter"

const SheetTitle = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Title>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Title>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <SheetPrimitive.Title
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-lg font-semibold text-foreground", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
SheetTitle.displayName = SheetPrimitive.Title.displayName

const SheetDescription = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Description>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SheetPrimitive.Description>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <SheetPrimitive.Description
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-sm text-muted-foreground", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
SheetDescription.displayName = SheetPrimitive.Description.displayName

export {
  Sheet,
  SheetPortal,
  SheetOverlay,
  SheetTrigger,
  SheetClose,
  SheetContent,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetFooter,
  SheetTitle,
  SheetDescription,
}
